# ####################
# Directive 76
# ####################

trait_libra_directive_76_a = {
	cost = {
		base = 0
		modifier = {
			add = 4
			nand = {
				is_variable_set = libra_aa_path
				check_variable = { which = libra_aa_path value = 10 }
			}
		}
	}
	category = advanced_genetic
	allowed_archetypes = { BIOLOGICAL LITHOID }
	tags = { organic special positive genetic_ascension pop_output army }
	initial = no
	randomized = no
	opposites = { "trait_libra_directive_76_b" "trait_libra_directive_76_c" }
	species_potential_add = {
		exists = from
		is_same_species_class = from
		from = {
			has_country_flag = libra_directive_76_species_trait
			is_variable_set = libra_aa_path
			check_variable = { which = libra_aa_path value = 10 }
		}
		nor = {
			has_trait = trait_latent_psionic
			has_trait = trait_psionic
			has_trait = trait_limited_cybernetic
			has_trait = trait_cybernetic
		}
	}
	species_possible_remove = {
		hidden_trigger = { exists = from }
		from = {
			has_ascension_perk = ap_libra_directive_76
		}
	}
	species_possible_merge_remove = { always = yes }
	modifier = {
		army_damage_mult = 0.5
		army_health = 0.5
		pop_cat_ruler_bonus_workforce_mult = 0.25
		specialist_and_complex_drone_cat_bonus_workforce_mult = 0.25
		soldier_jobs_bonus_workforce_mult = 0.25
	}
	slave_cost = {
		energy = 1000
	}
	assembly_score = {
		add = 2
	}
	ai_weight = {
		factor = 2
	}
}

leader_trait_libra_directive_76 = {
	force_councilor_trait = yes
	inline_script = {
		script = trait/libra_trait
		TYPE = genetic
		CLASS = leader
		ICON = "GFX_leader_trait_libra_directive_76"
		RARITY = veteran
		COUNCIL = triggered
		TIER = none
	}
	triggered_fleet_modifier = {
		potential = {
			is_ruler = no
			leader_class = commander
		}
		ship_fire_rate_mult = 0.25
		ship_weapon_damage = 0.35
	}
	triggered_army_modifier = {
		potential = {
			is_ruler = no
			leader_class = commander
		}
		army_morale = 0.25
		army_disengage_chance_mult = 0.35
	}
	triggered_self_modifier = {
		potential = {
			is_ruler = no
			leader_class = commander
		}
		planet_combat_width_add = 3
	}
	triggered_modifier = {
		potential = {
			is_ruler = no
			leader_class = scientist
		}
		science_ship_survey_speed = 0.25
		ship_anomaly_generation_chance_mult = 0.35
		ship_anomaly_research_speed_mult = 0.25
		ship_disengage_opportunities_add = 3
	}
	triggered_galcom_modifier = {
		potential = {
			is_ruler = no
			leader_class = official
		}
		diplo_weight_economy_mult = 0.25
		diplo_weight_pops_mult = 0.35
	}
	triggered_federation_modifier = {
		potential = {
			is_ruler = no
			leader_class = official
		}
		cohesion_ethics_penalty_mult = -0.25
		federation_fleet_cap_add = 350
	}
	planet_modifier = {
		planet_researchers_produces_mult = 0.25
	}
	triggered_planet_modifier = {
		potential = {
			is_ruler = no
			exists = owner
			owner = {
				is_variable_set = libra_aa_path
				check_variable = { which = libra_aa_path value = 10 }
			}
			if = {
				limit= { exists = from }
				from = {
					nor = {
						has_building = building_fe_clinic_1
						has_building = building_fe_clinic_2
					}
				}
			}
		}
		job_max_workforce_mult = 0.25
	}
	triggered_councilor_modifier = {
		potential = {
			is_ruler = yes
			exists = owner
			owner = {
				is_variable_set = libra_aa_path
				check_variable = { which = libra_aa_path value = 10 }
			}
		}
		planet_jobs_produces_mult = 0.1
	}
	leader_class = { commander official scientist }
	initial = no
	randomized = no
	replace_traits = { "leader_trait_psionic" "leader_trait_cyborg" "leader_trait_limited_cyborg" "leader_trait_virtual" "leader_trait_synthetic" "trait_leader_genetic_purist" }
}

leader_trait_libra_directive_76_clone = {
	force_councilor_trait = yes
	custom_subtitle = libra_common_genetic_leader_traits
	icon = {
		layer = {
			icon = "GFX_trait_bg"
			color = "trait_bg_negative"
		}
		layer = {
			icon = GFX_leader_trait_libra_directive_76
			color = "trait_icon_default"
		}
		inline_script = {
			script = trait/icon_element/council_triggered
			CLASS = leader
		}
		layer = {
			icon = GFX_trait_timed
		}
	}
	sorting_priority = @trait_sorting_priority_no_negative
	self_modifier = {
		leaders_upkeep_mult = -0.5
	}
	custom_tooltip_with_modifiers = leader_trait_libra_directive_76_clone_tt
	leader_class = { commander official scientist }
	initial = no
	randomized = no
	replace_traits = { "leader_trait_psionic" "leader_trait_cyborg" "leader_trait_limited_cyborg" "leader_trait_virtual" "leader_trait_synthetic" "trait_leader_genetic_purist" }
}

leader_trait_libra_directive_76_commander_a = {
	inline_script = {
		script = traits/libra_directive_76
		ICON = "GFX_leader_trait_libra_directive_76_commander_a"
	}
	fleet_modifier = {
		ship_armor_mult = 0.25
		ship_shield_mult = 0.25
	}
	triggered_fleet_modifier = {
		potential = {
			exists = owner
			owner = { is_gestalt = no }
		}
		custom_tooltip = leader_trait_libra_directive_76_commander_a_tt_1
		ship_fire_rate_mult = 0.04
		mult = owner.value:libra_ethic_destiny_trait_councilor_defense_mult
	}
	triggered_fleet_modifier = {
		potential = {
			exists = owner
			owner = { is_gestalt = yes }
		}
		custom_tooltip = leader_trait_libra_directive_76_commander_a_g_tt_1
		ship_fire_rate_mult = 0.04
		mult = owner.value:libra_ethic_destiny_trait_gestalt_legion_mult
	}
	triggered_army_modifier = {
		potential = {
			exists = owner
			owner = { is_gestalt = no }
		}
		custom_tooltip = leader_trait_libra_directive_76_commander_a_tt_2
		army_attack_damage_mult = 0.01
		army_attack_health_mult = 0.01
		mult = owner.value:libra_ethic_destiny_trait_councilor_defense_mult
	}
	triggered_army_modifier = {
		potential = {
			exists = owner
			owner = { is_gestalt = yes }
		}
		custom_tooltip = leader_trait_libra_directive_76_commander_a_g_tt_2
		army_attack_damage_mult = 0.01
		army_attack_health_mult = 0.01
		mult = owner.value:libra_ethic_destiny_trait_gestalt_legion_mult
	}
}

# ####################
# Purity
# ####################

trait_libra_purity_energy = {
	cost = {
		base = 4
		modifier = {
			subtract = 2
			is_variable_set = libra_aa_path
			has_tradition = tr_purity_finish
		}
	}
	initial = no
	randomized = no
	category = advanced_genetic
	allowed_archetypes = { BIOLOGICAL LITHOID }
	tags = { organic special positive genetic_ascension pop_output energy  }
	localized_tags = { SPECIES_TRAIT_LIBRA_PURITY }
	species_potential_add = { can_use_libra_purity_traits = yes }
	species_possible_remove = { can_remove_libra_purity_traits = yes }
	species_possible_merge_remove = { always = yes }
	modifier = {
		pop_housing_usage_mult = -0.1
		technician_jobs_bonus_workforce_mult = 0.4
	}
	slave_cost = {
		energy = 2000
	}
	assembly_score = {
		modifier = {
			add = 1.5
			from = { libra_has_generator_designation = yes }
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 0.5
			from = { libra_has_rural_designation = yes }
		}
	}
	ai_weight = {
		factor = 2
	}
}

	cost = {
		base = 4

		}
	}
	initial = no
	randomized = no
	category = advanced_genetic
	allowed_archetypes = { BIOLOGICAL LITHOID }
	tags = { organic special positive genetic_ascension pop_output trade  }
	localized_tags = { SPECIES_TRAIT_LIBRA_PURITY }

	species_possible_remove = { can_remove_libra_purity_traits = yes }
	species_possible_merge_remove = { always = yes }
	modifier = {
		pop_resettlement_cost_mult = -0.5
		trader_jobs_bonus_workforce_mult = 0.4
	}
	slave_cost = {
		energy = 2000
	}
	assembly_score = {
		modifier = {
			add = 1.5
			from = { libra_has_trade_designation = yes }
		}
	}
	ai_weight = {
		factor = 2
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			exists = from
			from = {
				libra_ai_preferences_trade = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

# ####################
# Evolutionary Endpoint
# ####################

leader_trait_libra_evolutionary_endpoint = {
	force_councilor_trait = yes
	inline_script = {
		script = trait/libra_trait
		TYPE = genetic
		CLASS = leader
		ICON = "GFX_leader_trait_libra_evolutionary_endpoint"
		RARITY = veteran
		COUNCIL = triggered
		TIER = none
	}
	triggered_fleet_modifier = {
		potential = {
			is_ruler = no
			leader_class = commander
		}
		ship_accuracy_mult = 0.25
		ship_tracking_mult = 0.25
	}
	triggered_army_modifier = {
		potential = {
			is_ruler = no
			leader_class = commander
		}
		army_morale = 0.25
		army_morale_damage_mult = 0.25
	}
	triggered_modifier = {
		potential = {
			is_ruler = no
			leader_class = scientist
		}
		science_ship_survey_speed = 0.25
		ship_anomaly_generation_chance_mult = 0.25
		ship_archaeological_site_excavation_speed_mult = 0.25
		ship_astral_rift_speed_mult = 0.25
	}
	triggered_galcom_modifier = {
		potential = {
			is_ruler = no
			leader_class = official
		}
		diplo_weight_technology_mult = 0.25
		diplo_weight_supporting_mult = 0.25
	}
	triggered_federation_modifier = {
		potential = {
			is_ruler = no
			leader_class = official
		}
		federation_experience_add = 2.5
		federation_fleet_cap_add = 350
	}
	triggered_planet_modifier = {
		potential = {
			exists = owner
			owner = {
				is_variable_set = libra_aa_path
				check_variable = { which = libra_aa_path value = 30 }
			}
		}
		planet_jobs_productive_produces_mult = 0.01
		planet_doctors_society_research_produces_add = 0.4
		planet_pop_assemblers_society_research_produces_add = 0.4
		mult = value:libra_ethic_destiny_trait_traits_mult
	}
	triggered_sector_modifier = {
		potential = {
			exists = owner
			owner = {
				is_variable_set = libra_aa_path
				check_variable = { which = libra_aa_path value = 30 }
			}
		}
		planet_jobs_productive_produces_mult = 0.005
		planet_doctors_society_research_produces_add = 0.2
		planet_pop_assemblers_society_research_produces_add = 0.2
		mult = value:libra_ethic_destiny_trait_traits_mult
	}
	triggered_councilor_modifier = {
		potential = {
			is_ruler = yes
			exists = owner
			owner = {
				is_variable_set = libra_aa_path
				check_variable = { which = libra_aa_path value = 30 }
			}
		}
		pop_environment_tolerance = 0.02
		country_physics_tech_research_speed = 0.03
		country_engineering_tech_research_speed = 0.03
		mult = value:libra_ethic_destiny_trait_traits_mult
	}
	custom_tooltip_with_modifiers = leader_trait_libra_traits_effect_tt
	leader_class = { commander official scientist }
	initial = no
	randomized = no
	on_gained_effect = { set_leader_flag = has_libra_extra_veteran_trait }
	replace_traits = { "leader_trait_psionic" "leader_trait_cyborg" "leader_trait_limited_cyborg" "leader_trait_virtual" "leader_trait_synthetic" "trait_leader_genetic_purist" }
}

trait_libra_cutefied = {
	sorting_priority = 20
	cost = {
		base = 5
		modifier = {
			subtract = 5
			is_variable_set = libra_aa_path
			has_ascension_perk = ap_libra_cutefied_world
			check_variable = { which = libra_aa_path value = 31 }
		}
	}
	initial = yes
	randomized = no
	immortal_leaders = no
	category = mutation
	allowed_archetypes = { LIBRACUTE }
	tags = { organic positive special species habitability leader pop_output }
	species_potential_add = { always = no }
	species_possible_remove = { always = no }
	species_possible_merge_remove = { always = no }
	modifier = {
		pop_bonus_workforce_mult = 0.4
		logistic_growth_mult = 0.4
		bonus_pop_growth_mult = 0.2
		pop_environment_tolerance = 0.2
		species_leader_exp_gain = 0.2
		leader_skill_add = 1
	}
	triggered_species_modifier = {
		potential = {
			exists = from
			from = { not = { has_ascension_perk = ap_libra_cutefied_world } }
		}
		pop_bonus_workforce_mult = -0.6
	}
	triggered_species_modifier = {
		potential = {
			or = {
				has_trait = trait_latent_psionic
				has_trait = trait_psionic
				has_trait = trait_limited_cybernetic
				has_trait = trait_cybernetic
			}
		}
		pop_bonus_workforce_mult = -0.6
	}
	resources = {
		category = planet_pops
		upkeep = {
			energy = @living_standard_energy_normal
			food = @living_standard_food_normal
		}
	}
	custom_tooltip_with_modifiers = trait_libra_cutefied_tooltip

	slave_cost = {
		energy = 2250
	}
	assembly_score = {
		base = 2
	}
}

leader_trait_libra_cutefied = {
	force_councilor_trait = yes
	inline_script = {
		script = trait/libra_trait
		TYPE = genetic
		CLASS = leader
		ICON = "GFX_leader_trait_libra_corrupt_official_governor_c"
		RARITY = veteran
		COUNCIL = triggered
		TIER = none
	}
	triggered_fleet_modifier = {
		potential = {
			leader_class = commander
			is_variable_set = libra_cutefied_level
		}
		ship_hull_regen_add_perc = 0.04
		ship_shield_regen_add_perc = 0.07
		ship_armor_regen_add_perc = 0.07
		mult = this.libra_cutefied_level
	}
	triggered_army_modifier = {
		potential = {
			leader_class = commander
			is_variable_set = libra_cutefied_level
		}
		army_damage_taken_mult = -0.04
		army_morale_damage_taken_mult = -0.07
		army_disengage_chance_mult = 0.07
		mult = this.libra_cutefied_level
	}
	triggered_self_modifier = {
		potential = {
			leader_class = scientist
			is_variable_set = libra_cutefied_level
		}
		leaders_upkeep_mult = -0.04
		leader_scientists_physics_research_produces_add = 6
		leader_scientists_society_research_produces_add = 6
		leader_scientists_engineering_research_produces_add = 6
		mult = this.libra_cutefied_level
	}
	triggered_galcom_modifier = {
		potential = {
			leader_class = official
			is_variable_set = libra_cutefied_level
		}
		resolutions_cost_mult = -0.04
		diplo_weight_pops_mult = 0.15
		mult = this.libra_cutefied_level
	}
	triggered_federation_modifier = {
		potential = {
			leader_class = official
			is_variable_set = libra_cutefied_level
		}
		federation_research_sharing_mult = 0.04
		federation_experience_add = 0.5
		mult = this.libra_cutefied_level
	}
	triggered_planet_modifier = {
		potential = {
			is_variable_set = libra_cutefied_level
			exists = owner
			owner = {
				is_variable_set = libra_aa_path
				check_variable = { which = libra_aa_path value = 31 }
			}
		}
		planet_jobs_productive_produces_mult = 0.06
		planet_jobs_upkeep_mult = -0.02
		mult = this.libra_cutefied_level
	}
	triggered_sector_modifier = {
		potential = {
			is_variable_set = libra_cutefied_level
			exists = owner
			owner = {
				is_variable_set = libra_aa_path
				check_variable = { which = libra_aa_path value = 31 }
			}
		}
		planet_jobs_productive_produces_mult = 0.03
		planet_jobs_upkeep_mult = -0.01
		mult = this.libra_cutefied_level
	}
	triggered_councilor_modifier = {
		potential = {
			is_ruler = yes
		}
		num_tech_alternatives_add = 1
	}
	triggered_councilor_modifier = {
		potential = {
			is_ruler = yes
			is_variable_set = libra_cutefied_level
			exists = owner
			owner = {
				is_variable_set = libra_aa_path
				check_variable = { which = libra_aa_path value = 31 }
			}
		}
		ships_logistics_mult = -0.025
		planet_pops_produces_mult = 0.025
		mult = this.libra_cutefied_level
	}
	triggered_desc = {
		trigger = { is_variable_set = libra_cutefied_level }
		text = leader_trait_libra_cutefied_effect_tt
	}
	on_gained_effect = {
		set_leader_flag = has_libra_extra_veteran_trait
		set_variable = {
			which = libra_cutefied_level
			value = 1
		}
	}
	leader_class = { commander official scientist }
	initial = no
	randomized = no
	replace_traits = { "leader_trait_erudite" "leader_trait_psionic" "leader_trait_cyborg" "leader_trait_limited_cyborg" "leader_trait_virtual" "leader_trait_synthetic" "trait_leader_genetic_purist" }
}

leader_trait_libra_multi_prosthesis_a = {
	inline_script = {
		script = trait/libra_traitc
		CLASS = leader
		ICON = "GFX_leader_trait_libra_multi_prosthesis_a"
		COUNCIL = triggered
		TIER = none
	}
	triggered_self_modifier = {
		potential = {
			leader_class = commander
			exists = owner
			owner = {
				is_variable_set = libra_aa_path
				check_variable = { which = libra_aa_path value = 40 }
			}
		}
		leader_trait_selection_options_add = 1
		leader_lifespan_mult = 0.15
		leaders_upkeep_mult = -0.25
		leader_commanders_alloys_produces_add = 8
	}
	triggered_self_modifier = {
		potential = {
			leader_class = scientist
			exists = owner
			owner = {
				is_variable_set = libra_aa_path
				check_variable = { which = libra_aa_path value = 40 }
			}
		}
		leader_trait_selection_options_add = 1
		leader_lifespan_mult = 0.15
		leaders_upkeep_mult = -0.25
		leader_scientists_alloys_produces_add = 8
	}
	triggered_self_modifier = {
		potential = {
			leader_class = official
			exists = owner
			owner = {
				is_variable_set = libra_aa_path
				check_variable = { which = libra_aa_path value = 40 }
			}
		}
		leader_trait_selection_options_add = 1
		leader_lifespan_mult = 0.15
		leaders_upkeep_mult = -0.25
		leader_officials_alloys_produces_add = 8
	}
	triggered_fleet_modifier = {
		potential = { leader_class = commander }
		ship_tracking_mult = 0.2
	}
	triggered_army_modifier = {
		potential = { leader_class = commander }
		army_damage_taken_mult = -0.1
	}
	triggered_modifier = {
		potential = { leader_class = scientist }
		ship_cloaking_strength_add = 2
		ship_disengage_opportunities_add = 2
	}
	triggered_galcom_modifier = {
		potential = { leader_class = official }
		diplo_weight_opposing_mult = 0.2
	}
	triggered_federation_modifier = {
		potential = { leader_class = official }
		federation_fleet_cap_add = 100
	}
	planet_modifier = {
		planet_soldiers_energy_produces_add = 4
		planet_enforcers_trade_produces_add = 2
	}
	sector_modifier = {
		planet_soldiers_energy_produces_add = 2
		planet_enforcers_trade_produces_add = 1
	}
	councilor_modifier = {
		country_naval_cap_mult = 0.05
	}
	leader_class = { commander scientist official }
	notify_on_gained = no
	opposites = { "leader_trait_libra_multi_prosthesis_b" "leader_trait_libra_multi_prosthesis_c" "leader_trait_libra_multi_prosthesis_d" "leader_trait_libra_multi_prosthesis_e" "leader_trait_libra_multi_prosthesis_f" }
}

		potential = { leader_class = official }
		leader_officials_physics_research_produces_add = 6
		leader_officials_society_research_produces_add = 8

		leader_scientists_society_research_produces_add = 6
		leader_scientists_engineering_research_produces_add = 8
		mult = value:libra_leader_trait_total_skill_mult
	}
	triggered_councilor_modifier = {
		potential = { has_base_skill = 10 }
		num_tech_alternatives_add = 1
		country_energy_produces_mult = 0.1
	}
	triggered_councilor_modifier = {
		potential = {
			exists = owner
			owner = {
				is_variable_set = libra_aa_path
				check_variable = { which = libra_aa_path value = 50 }
			}
		}
		country_physics_research_produces_mult = 0.005
		country_society_research_produces_mult = 0.005
		country_engineering_research_produces_mult = 0.005
		mult = value:libra_leader_trait_total_skill_mult

	custom_tooltip_with_modifiers = leader_trait_libra_total_skill_effect_tt

trait_libra_atomic_heart_energy = {
	cost = {
		base = 3
		modifier = {
			add = 2
			nand = {
				is_variable_set = libra_aa_path
				check_variable = { which = libra_aa_path value = 60 }
			}
		}
	}
	category = robotic
	allowed_archetypes = { MACHINE }
	localized_tags = { SPECIES_TRAIT_LIBRA_ATOMIC }
	tags = { machine modularity special positive pop_output energy }
	initial = no
	randomized = no
	opposites = { "trait_libra_atomic_heart_minerals" "trait_libra_atomic_heart_food" "trait_libra_atomic_heart_trade" "trait_libra_atomic_heart_consumer_goods" "trait_libra_atomic_heart_alloys" "trait_libra_atomic_heart_unity" "trait_libra_atomic_heart_research" "trait_libra_atomic_heart_war" "trait_libra_atomic_heart_influential" }
	species_potential_add = { can_use_libra_atomic_heart_traits = yes }
	species_possible_remove = { can_remove_libra_atomic_heart_traits = yes }
	species_possible_merge_remove = { always = yes }
	modifier = {
		technician_jobs_bonus_workforce_mult = 0.5
	}
	triggered_species_modifier = {
		potential = {
			exists = from
			from = {
				has_country_flag = libra_atomic_heart
				has_ascension_perk = ap_libra_atomic_heart
			}
		}
		technician_jobs_bonus_workforce_mult = 0.25
	}
	slave_cost = {
		energy = 2000
	}
	assembly_score = {
		modifier = {
			add = 1.5
			from = { libra_has_generator_designation = yes }
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 0.5
			from = { libra_has_rural_designation = yes }
		}
	}
	ai_weight = {
		factor = 2
	}
}

	inline_script = {
		script = trait/libra_trait
		TYPE = machine
		CLASS = official
		ICON = "GFX_leader_trait_libra_nanocore_official"
		RARITY = paragon
		COUNCIL = triggered
		TIER = none
	}
	federation_modifier = {
		cohesion_add = 2.5
	}
	triggered_federation_modifier = {
		potential = {
			is_variable_set = libra_nanocore_level
			exists = owner
			owner = {
				is_variable_set = libra_aa_path
				check_variable = { which = libra_aa_path value = 70 }
			}
		}
		federation_experience_add = 0.25
		mult = this.libra_nanocore_level
	}
	galcom_modifier = {
		diplo_weight_opposing_mult = 0.25
	}
	triggered_galcom_modifier = {
		potential = {
			is_variable_set = libra_nanocore_level
			exists = owner
			owner = {
				is_variable_set = libra_aa_path
				check_variable = { which = libra_aa_path value = 70 }
			}
		}
		diplo_weight_mult = 0.025
		mult = this.libra_nanocore_level
	}
	planet_modifier = {
		planet_jobs_produces_mult = 0.2
		planet_buildings_upkeep_mult = -0.2
	}
	triggered_planet_modifier = {
		potential = {
			is_variable_set = libra_nanocore_level
			exists = owner
			owner = {
				is_variable_set = libra_aa_path
				check_variable = { which = libra_aa_path value = 70 }
			}
		}
		planet_libra_nanite_buildings_produces_mult = 0.04
		bonus_automated_workforce_mult = 0.04
		mult = this.libra_nanocore_level
	}
	sector_modifier = {
		planet_jobs_produces_mult = 0.1
		planet_buildings_upkeep_mult = -0.1
	}
	triggered_sector_modifier = {
		potential = {
			is_variable_set = libra_nanocore_level
			exists = owner
			owner = {
				is_variable_set = libra_aa_path
				check_variable = { which = libra_aa_path value = 70 }
			}
		}
		planet_libra_nanite_buildings_produces_mult = 0.02
		bonus_automated_workforce_mult = 0.02
		mult = this.libra_nanocore_level
	}
	triggered_councilor_modifier = {
		potential = {
			is_ruler = yes
		}
		country_leader_cap_add = 1
	}
	triggered_councilor_modifier = {
		potential = {
			is_ruler = yes
			is_variable_set = libra_nanocore_level
			exists = owner
			owner = {
				is_variable_set = libra_aa_path
				check_variable = { which = libra_aa_path value = 70 }
			}
		}
		station_gatherers_produces_mult = 0.02
		species_leader_exp_gain = 0.02
		mult = this.libra_nanocore_level
	}
	triggered_desc = {
		trigger = { is_variable_set = libra_nanocore_level }
		text = leader_trait_libra_nanocore_effect_tt
	}
	on_gained_effect = {
		set_leader_flag = has_libra_extra_veteran_trait
		set_variable = {
			which = libra_nanocore_level
			value = 1
		}
	}
	leader_class = { commander scientist official }
	initial = no
	randomized = no
	notify_on_gained = no
	replace_traits = { "leader_trait_erudite" "leader_trait_psionic" "leader_trait_cyborg" "leader_trait_limited_cyborg" "leader_trait_synthetic" "leader_trait_virtual" "leader_trait_has_backup_clone" "trait_leader_genetic_purist" }
}

		mult = this.libra_nanocore_level
	}

			is_ruler = yes
		}
		country_leader_cap_add = 1
	}

		mult = this.libra_nanocore_level
	}
	triggered_desc = {
		trigger = { is_variable_set = libra_nanocore_level }
		text = leader_trait_libra_nanocore_effect_tt
	}
	on_gained_effect = {

			value = 1
		}
	}

	initial = no
	randomized = no
	notify_on_gained = no

				}
			}

		shipsize_nanite_swarmer_shield_add = 250

		shipsize_nanite_interdictor_shield_add = 5000
		shipsize_libra_nanite_titan_armor_add = 25000
		shipsize_libra_nanite_titan_shield_add = 25000

		}

		ship_shield_damage_mult = 0.02
